I scanned all the red-hot 3-character .si domains and picked out the 27 with the most investment value

.si is Slovenia's country suffix. This year it picked up a second meaning, because SI is the short form of super intelligence. In September the US government started using Super Intelligence and SI in place of AI in its own documents, and registrations jumped. Namecheap counted 3,234 new .si names on September 23 alone, and 46,066 across the whole of September.

So I scanned the lot. A three-character name, where each character is a letter or a number, has 36 options per position, which makes 46,656 combinations. I checked every one of them, and 24,827 can still be registered, a bit over half.

One finding surprised me. Every one of those 24,827 names mixes letters and digits. Not a single pure-letter name is left, out of 17,576 possible, and not a single pure-digit name either, out of 1,000 possible. If you want a three-character .si today, it will have a letter and a number in it, in some order.

Then I had AI go through the 24,827 and rank them by one simple test: the first two letters have to be an abbreviation people already use, tied to a real industry. Very little survived that test. The 27 that came out on top are these:

EV: ev3.si ev4.si ev5.si ev6.si ev8.si ev9.si
TV: tv4.si tv6.si tv7.si tv9.si
VR: vr2.si vr3.si vr4.si vr5.si vr6.si vr8.si vr9.si
AD: ad2.si ad4.si ad5.si ad6.si ad8.si
AR: ar3.si ar4.si ar5.si ar6.si
IP: ip2.si

Registering one is cheap right now, and holding one is cheap too. The cheapest I found is [Spaceship](https://spaceship.sjv.io/c/8075529/1794549/21274): the first year is $6.48 with the code SIREGSPS26, down from the regular $10.98, and the code works once per customer. Renewal after that is $11.98 a year, so a name costs about twelve dollars a year to keep. My own suggestion is to pick one you actually like, use it for a project if you have one, or hold it while the price of waiting is this low.

This list comes from my scan on October 30, 2026. A name on it can be registered by someone else at any time, so check at the registrar before you plan around one.

## The full list of 24,827 names

The names below are grouped by pattern, by where the letters and the digits sit. Inside each group they are in alphabetical order. The counts add up to 24,827, and every name appears exactly once.
